## Fixed calculation and scope rules

Version 1 denominator: **79 equal-weight leaf items** = 47 non-Split capability/acceptance units + 32 Split units. No parent/category/summary is scored again. NOT STARTED=0; IN PROGRESS=25; IMPLEMENTED=50; REGRESSION PASSED=75; ACCEPTED=100. BLOCKED retains the documented last evidenced score (P3-46=50), never an invented estimate. Overall = sum(row scores)/(79×100)×100; baseline 1975/7900=25.0%. Bar has 20 cells; floor(percentage/5) filled. Display one decimal, retain exact numerator. Scoring and weights stay fixed.

Every row has one status. REGRESSION PASSED means the named bounded/full regression passed, **not** all family/public/pixel/acceptance requirements. IMPLEMENTED requires actual relevant code but incomplete acceptance; declarations alone are NOT STARTED. An ordinary queued dependency is not a BLOCKED status. A genuine inability to advance names its defect/dependency and retained score. ACCEPTED requires the row acceptance condition plus every applicable shared exit condition and audit evidence. Consequently no historical bounded-only slice is promoted by this baseline. This conservative classification is explicit uncertainty about final acceptance, not loss of delivered engineering.

Shared primitive work is scored once in P3 rows. S3-F rows score only **Split-specific** admission/identity/construction/coverage tests and integration, never implementation of their referenced primitive again. Generic final gates score complete-matrix integration/proof, not the work inside individual primitive/action rows. S3-A checks are Split-specific; P3-42–47 cover the whole Phase 3 corpus. Reusing an artifact as evidence must not automatically advance both: each distinct condition must actually be proven.

Scope authority, in order: [approved rebuild Phase 3](ATME_VISUAL_PIPELINE_REBUILD.md#phase-3--renderer-and-action-runtime), [Remaining Matrix](ATME_VISUAL_PIPELINE_PHASE_3_REMAINING_MATRIX.md), [R-01–R-15](ATME_VISUAL_PIPELINE_TRACEABILITY.md), [approved Split specification](ATME_VISUAL_PIPELINE_PHASE_3_SPLIT_DESIGN.md), [reviewed implementation plan](superpowers/plans/2026-10-09-authored-visual-split.md). This ledger changes tracking only. It is now the current status authority; historical progress reports remain dated evidence.

New discoveries must record requirement, exact approved source, reason omitted, classification (existing-scope clarification versus expansion), proposed item/denominator delta, and decision in Scope changes below. No silent denominator increase or retrospective percentage rewrite. Actual scope expansion requires human review **before** it becomes a required row/exit blocker. Clarification inside an existing row adds evidence/acceptance detail without duplicate work; any necessary row split transparently preserves original total weight unless the human approves a new weighting version.

Out of Phase 3: semantic compiler/fallback direction (4), complete claim-to-developed-abstraction narrative workflow (5), creative choreography/read-listen prioritization (6), audible sound mix/intelligibility (7), final creative 16/20 rubric (8), MCP grammar (9), studio UX (10), automatic portrait recomposition and long-project/cache budgets (11), installed packaging/human release acceptance (12). Caption production/semantic timed text/layout follows the matrix's compiler/quality/studio/format ownership; it is not silently added here. Phase 3 still renders both **authored** profiles, validates sound-event timing, and proves renderer-level preview/export/offline behavior.

Each family has its own row/state above. Tasks1–9 now have bounded engineering acceptance, including all eight Task7 families. Tasks10–11 retain their approved dependencies and full requirements. Earlier partial adapter evidence is superseded by each family's complete bounded proof, without granting final shared/public/production acceptance. Kernel/family/production completion cannot substitute for one another.

## Phase 3 exit

100% requires every row ACCEPTED, supported primitive/action renderer preview/export parity, deterministic identical-input and random-seek equivalence, required actual pixel evidence, both authored profiles, relevant final regressions, explicit unsupported-action behavior, and independent final Phase 3 audit. Final installed/human/release gates remain later phases and cannot be claimed from this ledger. No rule, requirement, test, adapter or acceptance gate in the approved Split plan is waived.
